\displaystyle\leq(Bk\log n)^{k}\exp(-k\log n)+kB^{k}\exp(-k\log n)\sum_{m=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{\log^{m-1}n}(k\log n)^{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim(Bk\log n)^{k}\exp(-k\log n),

(176)

where the first inequality is proved by upper bounding the ratio between consecutive terms by
\frac{1}{\log n}
.
Putting the pieces together establishes the relation
(\mathrm{i})
and thus the Bernstein condition stated in (
C.2
).

Given that each
Z_{i}
satisfies the Bernstein-type condition (
C.2
),
by the power series expansion, we obtain,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\lambda Z_{i}\right)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{k}Z_{i}^{k}}{k!}\right]=1+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{k}Z_{i}^{k}}{k!}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\lambda^{k}Z_{i}^{k}\right]}{k!}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{k}\left(Bk\log n\right)^{k}}{k!}\left(\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\frac{e^{2}\lambda^{2}}{2}\left(B\log n\right)^{2}\left(\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\right),

for any
0<\lambda<\frac{1}{2eB\log n}
.
Here in the last step, we use the fact that for
x=\lambda B\log n\leq\frac{1}{2e}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{(kx)^{k}}{k!}~\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{i})}}{{\leq}}~\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi k}}\left(ex\right)^{k}\leq e^{2}x^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{2^{k}}=\frac{e^{2}x^{2}}{2},

where (i) follows from Stirling’s formula where
\sqrt{2\pi}k^{k+\frac{1}{2}}e^{-k}\leq k!
.
The above bound of the moment generating function leads naturally to a high probability control where
one can apply Markov’s inequality to arrive

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\min_{0<\lambda<\frac{1}{2eB\log n}}\Bigg\{\exp(-\lambda t)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\exp\Big(\lambda\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\Big)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\min_{0<\lambda<\frac{1}{2eB\log n}}\Bigg\{\exp(-\lambda t)\cdot\exp\Big(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{2}\lambda^{2}\left(B\log n\right)^{2}\big(\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\big)\Big)\Bigg\}.

Selecting
\lambda
according to

\displaystyle\lambda\asymp\min\left\{\frac{1}{B\log n},\frac{t}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(B\log n\right)^{2}\left(\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\Big)}\right\},

it is ensured that with probability at least
1-\delta/2
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\leq t\lesssim\max\left\{B\log n\log\frac{1}{\delta},~\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big(\mathsf{Var}(Z_{i})+\big(\frac{B\log n}{n}\big)^{2}\Big)\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\,\right\}.

(177)

Repeating the same argument above for
-Z_{i}
shows that the above inequality holds for
\sum_{i=1}^{n}-Z_{i}
.
Putting these together completes the proof of relation (
167
).

C.3
Proof of Lemma
4

Given any fixed
\theta\in\Theta
, independent of
\{X_{i}\}
, let us consider random variables
\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\frac{s\sigma}{n})
for
i\in[n]
.
In view of inequality (
168
),
\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\frac{s\sigma}{n})
forms a set of independent Bernoulli random variables with parameter smaller than
\frac{s}{n}.

Invoking Bernstein’s inequality (see, e.g.
(
Wainwright, 2019
, Chapter 2)
) ensures that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\left(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\frac{s\sigma}{n}\right)\leq s+\sqrt{2s\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+2\log\frac{1}{\delta},

(178)

with probability at least
1-\delta.

In order to deal with random
\widehat{\theta}
, consider an
\epsilon
-cover of
\Theta
of
\ell_{2}
-norm and denote it by
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
.
By virtue of the Lipschitz property of
h
, there exists some
\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
such that

\displaystyle|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})-h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|\leq L\epsilon,

which implies

\displaystyle\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<x_{i})\leq\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<x_{i}+L\epsilon),\qquad\text{for every }x_{i}

and hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\widehat{\theta}\in\Theta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<x_{i})\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<x_{i}+L\epsilon).

(179)

Before diving into our main proof, let us state a key property regarding
h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})
’s based on the above observation.
In particular, select parameters

\displaystyle x_{i}=\frac{\sigma s}{4n},\quad L\epsilon=\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},~\text{ and }~\delta=\frac{1}{n^{11}N(\epsilon,\Theta)}.

Taking a union bound of (
178
) over the
\epsilon
-cover
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sup_{\widehat{\theta}\in\Theta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<\frac{s\sigma}{4n}\Big)\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\frac{s\sigma}{4n}+L\epsilon\Big)\leq\frac{s}{3}+O\Big(\log N(\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},\Theta)\log n\Big),

(180)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11}).

In words, for every
\widehat{\theta}\in\Theta
and
s\gtrsim\log N(\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},\Theta)\log n
, the total number of index
i
, such that
|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<s\sigma/(4n)
is with high probability smaller than
s/2.

It implies that, if we rank the magnitude of
|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|
from the smallest to the largest, then regardless of the value of
\widehat{\theta}
, it always holds true with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{s}h^{2}_{(i)}(X_{i},\widehat{\theta})\geq\frac{s}{2}\cdot(\frac{s\sigma}{4n})^{2}=\frac{s^{3}\sigma^{2}}{n^{2}}.

(181)

We emphasize that (
181
) holds true for every
\widehat{\theta}
and
s
, as long as
s\gtrsim\log N(\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},\Theta)\log n.

We are now ready to establish the proof of (
169
).
For every
\varepsilon\in\real^{n}
and
\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
, let us define set

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\theta}:=\left\{i:|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon\right\}.

Then according to this definition, one naturally has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon)=|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|,

(182)

and as well as

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|}h_{(i)}^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|}(\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon)^{2}\lesssim\|\varepsilon\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{n^{4}},

(183)

where the last steps invokes the choice of
L\epsilon=\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}}.

If
|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|\lesssim\log N(\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},\Theta)\log n
for every
\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
, then it is straightforward to see that

\displaystyle\sup_{\widehat{\theta}\in\mathbb{B}(r)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<\varepsilon_{i}\Big)\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon\Big)=\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|\lesssim\log N(\frac{\sigma}{100n^{2}},\Theta)\log n.

Otherwise, taking collectively inequality (
183
) with inequality (
181
), one has

\displaystyle\forall\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon},\qquad\frac{|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|^{3}\sigma^{2}}{n^{2}}\lesssim\|\varepsilon\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{n^{4}},,

which further implies

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon)=|\mathcal{I}_{\theta}|\lesssim\left(\frac{n\|\varepsilon\|_{2}}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}.

(184)

Thus, in this case, we are guaranteed that

\displaystyle\sup_{\widehat{\theta}\in\mathbb{B}(r)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\widehat{\theta})|<\varepsilon_{i}\Big)\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\ind\Big(|h_{i}(X_{i};\theta)|<\varepsilon_{i}+L\epsilon\Big)\lesssim\left(\frac{n\|\varepsilon\|_{2}}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}.

(185)

Putting these two cases together completes the proof of property (
169
).

Appendix D
Proof of Lemma
2

D.1
A general statement

In this section, we prove a more general version of Lemma
2
without imposing the Assumption
1
. This general result reduces to Lemma
2
in the special case.

To simplify our statement, we start by introducing some auxiliary notation.
Specifically, let us define

\displaystyle\overline{\alpha}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\max_{k\leq t}\Big\{\left\|G_{k}(0)\right\|_{2},\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2},\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\Big\},

(186a)

\displaystyle\overline{\gamma}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\max_{k\leq t}\Big\{\left\|F_{k}(0)\right\|_{2},\|\gamma_{k}\|_{2},\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\Big\},

(186b)

and we write

\displaystyle\mu_{t}:=\frac{\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t-1}}{\|\alpha_{t-1}\|_{2}},\qquad\nu_{t}:=\frac{\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}{\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}},

(186c)

and

\displaystyle\overline{\xi}_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\Bigg(\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}^{2}\widehat{\gamma}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\Bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t},

(186d)

\displaystyle\overline{\zeta}_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\overline{\gamma}_{t}\Bigg(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{G}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\Bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}.

(186e)

Our goal is to establish the following claim in order to control the sizes of
\xi_{t}
and
\zeta_{t}
.
This claim is of the same form as in Claim
1
.

Claim 2
.

There exists universal constant
0<c<1
, such that the following set of inequalities hold true

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\right),

(187a)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right),

(187b)

\displaystyle|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n^{2}}}\rho_{1,F}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t-1}\right)+\rho_{F}\left(\frac{\mu_{t}^{2}t\log^{2}n}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},

(187c)

\displaystyle|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n^{2}}}\rho_{1,G}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\right)+\rho_{G}\left(\frac{\nu_{t}^{2}t\log^{2}n}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},

(187d)

\displaystyle|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}(1-c)^{t-k-1}\Big|\widehat{\alpha}_{\frac{t+k-1}{2}}^{\frac{t+k-1}{2}}\Big|&\text{if}&t-1-k=2m,\\[11.38092pt]
(1-c)^{t-k-2}\rho_{F}^{2}\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{\frac{t+k}{2}}^{\frac{t+k}{2}}\Big|&\text{if}&t-1-k=2m+1,\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}(1-c)^{\frac{t-k}{2}}|\widehat{\gamma}_{\frac{t+k}{2}}^{\frac{t+k}{2}}|&\text{if}&t-k=2m,\\
(1-c)^{\frac{t-k}{2}}\rho_{G}^{2}|\widehat{\alpha}_{\frac{t+k-1}{2}}^{\frac{t+k-1}{2}}|&\text{if}&t-1-k=2m.\end{array}\right.

Let us state an inductive results regarding the above Claim
2
. The proof of this result is provided in Section
D.2
.

Lemma 6
.

Under the decomposition (
22
) with (
53
), the bound (
187
) holds for
t=1

with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
.
In addition, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, for every
t
satisfying

\displaystyle t\ll\frac{n}{\rho_{F}^{4}\rho_{G}^{4}\log^{4}n},

(188)

if the bound (
187
) and Assumption
3
below hold for
t
, then the bound (
187
) holds for
t+1
.

Assumption 3
.

For the decomposition (
22
) with (
53
), assume the following conditions hold:

•

there exists some universal constant
0<c<1/2
, such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]

\displaystyle<(1-2c)^{2},

(189)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t+1}\|_{2}\big]

\displaystyle<(1-2c)^{2}.

(190)

•

for some universal constant
0\leq c<1
, such that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|,|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\leq c^{t-k}\mathsf{poly}(n),

(191a)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{1,F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\lesssim\rho_{F}\lesssim\mathsf{poly}(n)\qquad\text{and}\qquad\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{1,G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\lesssim\rho_{G}\lesssim\mathsf{poly}(n),

(191b)

\displaystyle\rho_{G}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|\overline{\gamma}_{t}\ll\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\overline{\alpha}_{t}\ll\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2};

(191c)

•

in addition, we assume

\displaystyle\rho_{F}\rho_{G}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|\overline{\xi}_{k-1}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}+\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right),

(192a)

\displaystyle\rho_{F}^{2}\rho_{G}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\overline{\zeta}_{k-1}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right),

(192b)

\displaystyle\rho_{1,G}\sqrt{\frac{t\log n}{n}}\overline{\xi}_{t}+\rho_{G}\sqrt{\log n}\left(\frac{\overline{\xi}_{t}}{\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{F}},

(192c)

\displaystyle\rho_{1,F}\sqrt{\frac{t\log n}{n}}\overline{\zeta}_{t}+\rho_{F}\sqrt{\log n}\left(\frac{\overline{\zeta}_{t}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}},

(192d)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\rho_{G}\left[\sqrt{n}\rho_{1,G}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}^{2}\widehat{\gamma}_{t-1}^{t-1})+\rho_{G}n\left(\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}^{2}\widehat{\gamma}_{t-1}^{t-1})}{\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{F}^{2}},

(192e)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\left[\sqrt{n}\rho_{1,F}\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})+\rho_{F}n\left(\frac{\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}}.

(192f)

Remark 10
.

Under the Assumptions
1
and
2
, and further assuming that the Claim
1
holds true at
t
, it is straightforward to verify Assumption
3
. Crucially, taking
\rho_{1,F},\rho_{1,G}=0
,
\rho_{F},\rho_{G}\asymp 1
and
\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2},\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\asymp 1
helps simplify the presentations of the above formulas to a large extent.

D.2
Proof of Lemma
6

Before diving into the proof of Lemma
6
, let us first state a few preliminaries.
Throughout this proof, we condition on the event

\displaystyle\left\|(\phi_{1},\ldots,\phi_{t-1})^{\top}(\phi_{1},\ldots,\phi_{t-1})-I_{t-1}\right\|_{\textsf{op}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log\frac{n}{\delta}}{n}},\qquad\text{for every }1<t\leq n,

(193)

\displaystyle\text{and }

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{n}{p}(\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{t-1})^{\top}(\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{t-1})-I_{t-1}\right\|_{\textsf{op}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log\frac{p}{\delta}}{p}},\qquad\text{for every }1<t\leq n,

both of which hold with probability at least
1-\delta
according to inequalities (
171
) and (
172b
); in this proof, we shall take
\delta=n^{-11}.

In addition, the following results turn out to be essential for our analysis, whose proof is deferred to Section
E
.

Lemma 7
.

Under the assumptions (
191b
) – (
188
), the following two inequalities hold true with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
:

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right),

(194)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t+1}b_{k}\Big[\left\langle\phi_{k},G_{t+1}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\rangle\gamma_{t+1}^{k}-\sum_{j=k}^{t}\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{j}\left\langle G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\circ G_{j}^{\prime}\left(u_{j}\right)\right\rangle\gamma_{j}^{k}\Big]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}\right).

(195)

Initial case for
t=1
.

In order to prove inequalities (
187
) for
t=1
, first recall the initialization condition that

\displaystyle s_{1}=r_{1}-\epsilon=X{\theta}^{\star}.

By construction of (
61
) and (
64a
),
\phi_{1}={\theta}^{\star}/\|{\theta}^{\star}\|_{2}
and
\gamma^{1}_{1}=\|{\theta}^{\star}\|_{2}
and hence
\xi_{1}=\widehat{\xi}_{1}=0.

In addition, since
\widehat{s}_{1}=s_{1}
, it is guaranteed that

\displaystyle\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\langle G_{1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{1})-G_{1}^{\prime}(s_{1})\rangle+\frac{1}{\|\gamma_{1}\|_{2}}\langle\phi_{1},G_{1}(s_{1})-G_{1}(\widehat{s}_{1})\rangle=0.

As a result, inequalities (
187a
) and (
187d
) hold for
t=1
. The requirements for coefficient
|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}|
naturally holds as they equal to zero when
t=0.

It suffices to establish the required result for
\|\widehat{\zeta}_{1}\|_{2}
. Towards this, by construction (
63
) and definition (
70
), we observe that

\displaystyle\alpha^{1}_{1}

\displaystyle=\langle G_{1}(s_{1}),\,a_{1}\rangle=\|G_{1}(s_{1})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\zeta}_{1}=b_{1}\left[\left\langle\phi_{1},G_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle-\langle G_{1}^{\prime}\rangle\gamma_{1}^{1}-\alpha_{1}^{1}q_{1}^{1}\right].

To obtain a control of the right hand side of
\widehat{\zeta}_{1}
, expression (
7
) of Lemma
7
— whose assumptions satisfy naturally as both
\widehat{\alpha}_{0}
and
\widehat{\gamma}_{1}^{1}
vanish — ensures that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\zeta}_{1}\|_{2}=\left|\left\langle\phi_{1},G_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle-\langle G_{1}^{\prime}\rangle\gamma_{1}^{1}-\alpha_{1}^{1}q_{1}^{1}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}\left(\overline{\alpha}_{1}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{1}\right),

(196)

from which, we complete the proof of (
187b
).

Inductive relation.

Suppose both Assumption
3
and the target conclusion (
187
) hold at the
t
-th iteration.
We shall prove the inequality set (
187
) at the
t+1
-th iteration.
First, we remark that given (
187
) holds at iteration
t
, decomposition (
53a
) leads to

\displaystyle\|\xi_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|\|G_{k}(s_{k})\|_{2}+\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\mathrm{i})}}{{\lesssim}}\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|\cdot\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\mathrm{ii})}}{{\lesssim}}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\Bigg(\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}^{2}\widehat{\gamma}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\Bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}=:\overline{\xi}_{t}.

(197)

Here
(\mathrm{i})
invokes the relation that
\|G_{t}(s_{t})\|_{2}=\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}
and the inductive assumption (
187a
);
(\mathrm{ii})
follows from the geometric decay of
\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{k}
in expression (
187
).
Similarly, in view of expression (
53b
), one has

\displaystyle\|\zeta_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\|F_{k}(\beta_{k})\|_{2}+\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\cdot\|\gamma_{k}\|_{2}+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\overline{\gamma}_{t}\Bigg(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{G}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\Bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}=:\overline{\zeta}_{t}

(198)

In addition, we obtain in the similar fashion that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{s}_{t}-u_{t}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}G_{k}(u_{k})\Big\|_{2}\lesssim\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1}+\rho_{F}^{2}\widehat{\gamma}_{t-1}^{t-1})\leq\overline{\xi}_{t},

(199)

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(v_{k})\Big\|_{2}\lesssim\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})\leq\overline{\zeta}_{t}.

(200)

With these control in place, let us verify the induction results for the next iteration based on Assumption
3
.

D.2.1
Induction step for quantities
\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}
and
\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

Let us start by showing that expression (
187a
) holds at the
t+1
-th iteration.
In view of expression (
54
), it directly satisfies that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big\|\mathcal{P}_{G_{t}(s_{t})}^{\perp}\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle\Big\|_{2}+O\left(\sqrt{\frac{t\log n}{n}}\|\gamma_{t+1}\|_{2}\right).

(201)

It is then sufficient to control the above two terms on the right accordingly.
Recall that Lemma
7
ensures that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right),

(202)

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t+1}b_{k}\Big[\left\langle\phi_{k},G_{t+1}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\rangle\gamma_{t+1}^{k}-\sum_{j=k}^{t}\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{j}\left\langle G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\circ G_{j}^{\prime}\left(u_{j}\right)\right\rangle\gamma_{j}^{k}\Big]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}\right).

(203)

which completes the control of the first term.

Regarding the second term, recall that
\{a_{k}\}
(defined in expression (
62
)) forms a set of orthonormal basis.
There exists a unit vector
w\in G_{t}(s_{t})^{\perp}\cap\mathsf{span}\{a_{k}\}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{G_{t}(s_{t})}^{\perp}\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=w^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{G_{t}(s_{t})}^{\perp}\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}a_{k},\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle\right\rangle,

(204)

where
\omega\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1}
for
\omega_{k}=w^{\top}a_{k}
.
In view of this decomposition, we remark that as stated in (
A.2
), one has

\displaystyle 0=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}a_{k},\sum_{k=1}^{t}\alpha_{t}^{k}a_{k}\right\rangle=\langle\omega,\,\alpha_{t}\rangle.

(205)

In addition, there exists some
\overline{\zeta}\in\real^{p}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}_{G_{t}(s_{t})}^{\perp}\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\circ\left(\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}\left\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}.

(206)

Therefore, it is enough to control the two terms on the right hand side above, which is what shall be done in the following.

•

Control of
\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}
.
Here, the size of
\overline{\zeta}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle\|\overline{\zeta}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}+\|\beta_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}+\|\zeta_{t}\|_{2}\lesssim\overline{\zeta}_{t},

(207)

where the last relation follows from the derivations in (
D.2
) and (
200
).
Putting the pieces above together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}\left\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big).

(208)

To further control the right hand side, recall the definitions that
\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}=v_{t+1}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(v_{k})
and

\beta_{t+1}=v_{t+1}+\zeta_{t}=v_{t+1}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(\beta_{k})+\widehat{\zeta}_{t}
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}(F_{k}(\beta_{k})-F_{k}(v_{k}))\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big).

(209)

Here, in the last inequality, we make the observation that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}(F_{k}(\beta_{k})-F_{k}(v_{k}))\Big\|_{2}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\rho_{F}\|\zeta_{k-1}\|_{2}\leq\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\overline{\zeta}_{k-1}\lesssim\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right),

where the last inequality follows from the assumptions (
192b
) and
\rho_{G}\geq 1.

Now invoking our inductive assumption (
187b
) and the assumption that
\rho_{F},\rho_{G}\geq 1
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right).

(210)

As a result, it can be concluded that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}\Big(\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)\Big)+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}O\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Bigg),

(211)

where the last line follows since we condition on event (
193
).

•

Control of
\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\|_{2}
.

Next, we shall focus our attention on quantity
\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\|_{2}

with the size of
\overline{\zeta}
bounded by (
207
).
To begin with, the property (
278a
) summarized in Lemma
10
ensures

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{1,F}\sqrt{\frac{t\log n}{n}}\|\overline{\zeta}\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\left(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}+\sqrt{\log n}\left(\frac{\left\|\overline{\zeta}\right\|_{2}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}}.

(212)

Here in the last inequality, we invoke the assumptions (
188
) and (
192d
).

Now in view of triangle’s inequality, in order to bound the size of
\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\|
, it is sufficient to control the size of
\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\|_{2}.

Towards this goal, we introduce the following lemma.

Lemma 8
.

With probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, for any
\omega\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\Bigg\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}^{2},

(213a)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\rho_{F}^{2}.

(213b)

The proof of this lemma is postponed to Section
F.2
.

Combining (
213a
) with the assumption (
188
), which suggests
\rho_{F}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\ll 1/\rho_{G}^{2}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\Bigg\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}}.

Taking this collectively with (
212
), it is ensured that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1}+\overline{\zeta})\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}}\Big)+\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}}\Big).

(214)

Similarly, one can also conclude that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\mu_{k}\phi_{k}\circ G_{t}^{\prime}(u_{t}+\overline{\xi})\Big\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\Big).

(215)

In summary.

With these properties in place, we are ready to bound
\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}.
Recall expression (
211
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}}\Big)\Bigg)\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big),

(216)

where we make use of the relation (
214
). Similar to (
216
), one can establish the recursive relation between
\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}
and
\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}
as in

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\zeta}_{t}\|_{2}\leq\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\Big)\Bigg)\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right)\Big).

Consequently, the above two relations combined together yields

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\xi}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}}\Big)\Bigg)\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o\Big(\frac{1}{\rho_{F}}\Big)\Bigg)\cdot\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]}+o(1)\Bigg)\cdot\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\Big(1-c\Big)\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}+O\Big(\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\right)\Big),

(217)

where the last inequality follows from the assumption (
189
).

Putting inequality (
D.2.1
) and the inductive assumption (
187a
) that
\|\widehat{\xi}_{t}\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\right)
,
we complete the proof of (
187a
) at
t+1.

Additionally, the control of
\widehat{\zeta}_{t+1}
can be derived in a similar way, which we omit here for brevity.

D.2.2
Induction step for quantity
|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{t}|
and
|\widehat{\gamma}_{t+1}^{t+1}|

Let us recall our definition of
\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{t}
in expression (
–
‣
•
‣
3.1
) where

\displaystyle\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{t}\coloneqq\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\beta_{t+1})\rangle+\frac{1}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}^{2}}\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\alpha_{t}^{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle.

To establish (
187c
) at the
t+1
-th iteration, it is sufficient to bound the two terms on right of the above expression respectively.

To begin with, according to inequality (
278c
) in Lemma
10
, we are ensured that

\displaystyle\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\beta_{t+1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\rho_{1,F}\left\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\left(\frac{t\log n}{n}+\left(\frac{\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n^{2}}}\rho_{1,F}\left(\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}\right)+\rho_{F}\left(\frac{\mu_{t+1}^{2}t\log^{2}n}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}.

where we invoke the relation (
210
) and recall that

\mu_{t+1}:=\frac{\rho_{F}\rho_{G}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\rho_{G}\overline{\gamma}_{t}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}
as in expression (
186c
).
In addition, conditioning on event (
193
), it is easily seen that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}^{2}}\left\langle\sum_{k}\alpha_{t}^{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\beta_{t+1})-F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}^{2}}\Big\|\sum_{k}\alpha_{t}^{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}\cdot\rho_{F}\left\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{F}\frac{\left\|\beta_{t+1}-\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\right\|_{2}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\lesssim\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{t+1}^{2}t\log^{2}n}{n}}.

Combining these two bounds establishes (
187c
) for
\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{t}
.
Moreover, the upper bound of
\widehat{\gamma}_{t+1}^{t+1}
as in (
187d
) can be derived in a similar manner, thus is omitted here.

D.2.3
Induction step for quantities
|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k}|

For
k<t
, recall the definitions in (
–
‣
•
‣
3.1
) and (
52
) that

\displaystyle\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k+1}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\widehat{\gamma}_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{k}\left\langle G_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t+1})\circ G_{k}^{\prime}(u_{k})\right\rangle

When
k=t-1
, it is easily seen that
|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{t-1}|\coloneqq|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{t}^{\prime}(v_{t})\rangle|\leq\rho^{2}_{F}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}|.

So we only need to prove for
k\leq t-2
where

\displaystyle\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k+1}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle=\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k+1}\left\langle G_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t})\circ G_{k+1}^{\prime}(u_{k+1})\right\rangle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle.

(218)

Before proceeding, let us make note of the simple relation that

\displaystyle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|_{1}.

(219)

We shall bound the two parts on the right respectively as below.

•

In view of Lemma
10
inequality (
278b
), it satisfies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\Bigg[\sqrt{n}\rho_{1,F}\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\Bigg(t\log n+n\Bigg(\frac{\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\Bigg)^{\frac{2}{3}}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\Bigg[\sqrt{n}\rho_{1,F}\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})+\rho_{F}n\Bigg(\frac{\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})}{\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}}\Bigg)^{\frac{2}{3}}\Bigg]+\frac{t\log n\rho^{2}_{F}}{n}

(220)

where the last step follows from the inequality (
200
) where we proved
\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-v_{t+1}\|_{2}\leq\overline{\gamma}_{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{t}+\rho_{G}^{2}\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{t-1})
.
Next, by virtue of assumptions (
192f
) and (
188
),
the right hand side of inequality (
•
‣
D.2.3
) further satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\rho_{F}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|_{1}\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}}.

(221)

•

It is then sufficient to consider the quantity
\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\rangle.

Towards this, it is easily seen that

\displaystyle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle\leq\frac{1}{n}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|_{2}\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\|_{2}.

To bound the right hand side, notice that according to Lemma
8
, for every
k
, it obeys

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{k+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}\big]\lesssim\rho_{F}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}\ll\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}}.

Therefore we arrive at

\displaystyle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(v_{t+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]+o(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}})}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{k+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}\big]+o(\frac{1}{\rho_{G}^{2}})}

Taking the above inequality collectively with displays (
219
) and (
221
) gives us

\displaystyle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{t+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|F_{k+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{v}_{k+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}\big]}+o(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}_{G}}).

(222)

Similarly, one can develop a symmetric bound for
G^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle\left\langle G_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{s}_{t})\circ G_{k+1}^{\prime}(u_{k+1})\right\rangle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{t}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{t}\|_{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|G_{k+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{u}_{k+1})\right\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,\|\gamma_{k+1}\|_{2}\big]}+o(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}_{F}}).

(223)

Therefore, under assumption (
189
), it holds that

\displaystyle\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{k+1}^{\prime}(v_{k+1})\right\rangle\left\langle G_{t}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{s}_{t}\right)\circ G_{k+1}^{\prime}\left(u_{k+1}\right)\right\rangle<(1-c)^{2}.

As a consequence, we can establish the inductive relationship that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\leq(1-c)^{2}|\widehat{\alpha}_{t-1}^{k+1}|.

Invoking the above inequality recursively validates the relation (
187
) at
t+1
-th step.
In addition, one can prove for inequality (
187
) in a similar manner.

Appendix E
Proof of Lemma
7

Let us present the proof of inequality (
7
) and inequality (
7
) can be established in the same fashion.
Throughout this proof, let us condition on the event where both expressions (
172a
) and (
172b
) hold true (with
\delta
chosen as
\max(n,p)^{-11}
)

\displaystyle\left\|(\phi_{1},\ldots,\phi_{t-1})^{\top}(\phi_{1},\ldots,\phi_{t-1})-I_{t-1}\right\|_{\text{op}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log n}{n}},\qquad\text{for every }1<t\leq n,

(224a)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{n}{p}(\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{t-1})^{\top}(\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{t-1})-I_{t-1}\right\|_{\text{op}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}},\qquad\text{for every }1<t\leq n,

(224b)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
.

We are ready to control the norm on the left hand side of inequality (
7
). First, recalling that
\{a_{k}\}
forms an orthogonal basis,
therefore there exists a unit vector

\displaystyle\omega\coloneqq\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}a_{k}\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1},

(225)

— depending on the randomness of
\{\psi_{k}\}
— such that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\omega^{\top}\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Bigg\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\Bigg\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\cdot\omega^{\top}\alpha_{t}-\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}^{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Y_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Z_{i},

(226)

where to simplify our presentation, we introduce the following short-hand notation

\displaystyle X_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right]_{i}

(227a)

\displaystyle X_{i}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left[\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ\Bigg(F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Bigg)\right]_{i}

(227b)

\displaystyle Y_{i}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{n}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right]_{i}\cdot\omega^{\top}\alpha_{t}

(227c)

\displaystyle Z_{i}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}.

(227d)

Here, in view of definitions in (
52
), we remind the readers that, in these expressions above, the parameters concerned are

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}\coloneqq\Big\{\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widehat{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big\},\quad\text{where }\omega\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1},\alpha_{k}\in\real^{k},\widehat{\gamma}_{t}\in\real^{t},\text{ and }\tau_{k}\in\real^{s}.

(228)

Let us point out a few properties for the above parameters:

•

\tau_{k}
corresponds to the parameter used for defining function
F_{k}
. By assumption,
\tau_{k}
is of finite and low dimension and
\|\tau_{k}\|_{2}\leq c
for some universal constant;

•

according to the assumption (
191b
),

\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}\leq\overline{\alpha}_{t}\leq\mathsf{poly}(n)
, for every
k\leq t
;

•

in view of the assumption (
191a
),
\|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{t}(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k})^{2}\leq\mathsf{poly}(n)/(1-c^{2}).

The value of
\widehat{\theta}
depends on the randomness in
\{\psi_{k}\}
; we collect all the possible values of
\widehat{\theta}
to be space
\Theta
, namely,

\displaystyle\Theta\coloneqq\Big\{(\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widehat{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1})~\big|~\omega\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1},\|\tau_{k}\|_{2}\leq 1,\|\alpha_{k}\|_{2}\leq\mathsf{poly}(n),\|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\mathsf{poly}(n)}{1-c^{2}}\Big\}.

(229)

Next, let us control the right hand side of inequality (
226
), which shall be done by bounding each term in the summation separately.

E.1
Controlling (
227a
)

Regarding the first term
\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}^{0}
, since
(\omega,\widehat{\gamma})
has complicated statistical dependence on the randomness of
\{\psi_{k}\}
, we find it helpful to construct an
\epsilon
-covering set
\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
of the space
\mathcal{S}^{t-1}(1)\times\mathcal{S}^{t-1}(\mathsf{poly}(n))
for
\epsilon=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}
, with its cardinality satisfying

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}|\lesssim\Big(\frac{\mathsf{poly}(n)}{\epsilon}\Big)^{t}=\mathsf{poly}(n)^{t}.

Before diving into the main proof, we make note of the following property

\displaystyle\Bigg\|F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|F_{t+1}(0)\right\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|F_{t+1}(0)\right\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\cdot\left\|F_{k}(0)\right\|_{2}\lesssim\overline{\gamma}_{t+1},

(230)

where in the last inequality, we invoke assumption (
191c
) which implies
\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\ll 1.

Given every
(\omega,\widehat{\gamma}_{t})\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1}(1)\times\mathcal{S}^{t-1}(\mathsf{poly}(n))
, there exists
(\widetilde{\omega},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t})\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}
satisfying
\|\widetilde{\omega}-\omega\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}-\widehat{\gamma}_{t}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}
. This fact together with the Lipschitz property of function
F_{t}
gives

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\rangle-\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}\Big\|F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)-F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\|_{2}+\Big\|F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\|_{2}\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}-\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\mathrm{i})}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\rho_{F}\|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}\|_{2}\overline{\gamma}_{t}+\|\omega-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\cdot\epsilon\cdot\rho_{F}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1},

(231)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11}).

Here,
\mathrm{(i)}
follows from the fact that we condition on the event (
224
).
Putting things together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{(\widetilde{\omega},\widetilde{\gamma})\in\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}.

To further control the right hand side above, let us make note of the following two observations that
(i)
\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right\rangle

is stochastically dominated by
\mathcal{N}(0,\frac{\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}^{2}}{n})
and (ii) the standard concentration result (see, e.g.
(
Wainwright, 2019
, Exercise 2.12)
).

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{i\in[k]}X_{i}-\sqrt{2\sigma^{2}\log k}\geq t\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}},

(232)

for
X_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\textrm{i.i.d}}}{{\sim}}\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}).

Consequently, we conclude that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log(n)}{n}}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1},

(233)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11}).

E.2
Controlling the remaining terms

For notational simplicity, if we concatenate
\{\psi_{k}\}_{k=1}^{t}
into matrix
\Psi
, namely,

\displaystyle\Psi\coloneqq\big[\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{t}\big]\in\real^{p\times t},\qquad\text{where }\psi_{k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{i.i.d.}}}{{\sim}}\mathcal{N}(0,\frac{1}{n}I_{p}),

(234)

it suffices to control the summation of the remaining three terms as

\displaystyle H(\Psi;\widehat{\theta})\coloneqq\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Y_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Z_{i}.

(235)

For any fixed parameter
\theta\in\Theta
and fixed
i\in[p]
, it is easily seen from definitions (
227
), random vector
(X_{i},Y_{i},Z_{i})
only depends on the
i
-th row of
\Psi
matrix, namely,
[\psi_{k,i}]_{k=1}^{t}
, which implies that
\{(X_{i},Y_{i},Z_{i})\}_{i=1}^{p}
are independent for different
i
.
In addition, in view of Stein’s lemma of Gaussian random vectors — which ensures
\mathbb{E}_{X\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\big[Xf(X)\big]=\mathbb{E}_{X\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\big[f^{\prime}(X)\big]
— one can verify

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[H(\Psi;\theta)\big]=0.

(236)

However, the above property holds only when
\theta
is held as a fixed vector, independent of
\{\psi_{k}\}.

When a random
\widehat{\theta}
is concerned, due to the statistical dependence between
\widehat{\theta}
and
(X_{i},Y_{i},Z_{i})
’s, the mean zero property does not hold anymore.

On the high level, to control
H(\Psi;\widehat{\theta})
, the idea is to invoke Lemma
3
to bound
H(\Psi;\theta)
for each fixed
\theta\in\Theta
, which is achieved via Step 1-3 below.
In Step 4, we develop a uniform control of
H(\Psi;\theta)
over the space
\Theta
in order to deal with the statistical dependence involved in
\widehat{\theta}.

In order to apply Lemma
3
, it boils down to computing the variance of
H(\Psi;\theta)
and validating the property (
166
), as shall be done as follows.

Step 1: variances control.

We claim that for every fixed
\theta\in\Theta
(independent of
\{\psi_{k}\}
), the variance terms satisfy the following relations respectively

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}^{2}+\rho_{F}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2})

(237a)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}^{2}}{n^{2}}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(237b)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(Z_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}^{2}}{n^{2}}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

(237c)

Given the above relations, the variance of
H(\Psi;\theta)
satisfies

\displaystyle\operatorname{var}(H(\Psi;\theta))\lesssim\frac{\log n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}^{2}+\rho_{F}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}).

Let us establish these three claims respectively.
We remark that throughout this step,
\theta
should always be viewed as a fixed constant that does not dependent on any randomness of the problem. With a slight abuse of notation, we still write parameters such as
\alpha_{t}
,
\widehat{\beta}_{t}
,
\widehat{\gamma}_{t}
, but here they should be understood as fixed parameters.

•

First, by noticing
|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})|\leq\rho_{F}
and
|\omega^{\top}\alpha_{t}|\leq\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(Y_{i})\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})]_{i}^{2}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}^{2}}{n}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2},

which establishes relation (
237b
).

•

In terms of the relation (
237c
), for each fixed
\theta\in\Theta
, we remind the readers that
[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)]_{i}
are independent of each other. This fact leads to

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(Z_{i})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}\Bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}\Big)\cdot\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\Bigg)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\mathsf{Var}\Bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}\Big)\cdot\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\Bigg)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}\Big)\cdot\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\Bigg]^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big|\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}\cdot\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\Big|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\Bigg]^{2}},

where the last used the basic property that
\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\sum_{i}X_{i}]^{2}}\leq\sum_{i}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[X_{i}^{2}]}.

To avoid confusion, we remind the readers that the parameters here are treated as fixed and independent of the randomness in the problem.
Now, in view of the basic inequality
|\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}|\leq\|\alpha_{j}\|_{2}
, we can further bound

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(Z_{i})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\cdot\|\alpha_{j}\|_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\|\alpha_{j}\|_{2}\rho_{F}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}}{n}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2},

(238)

where the last inequality invokes the assumption (
191c
).
This completes the proof of inequality (
237c
).

•

When it comes to the inequality (
237a
), basic inequality ensures that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i})\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i}^{0}+X_{i})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i}^{0}).

We shall compute these two terms on the right respectively.
Note that for fixed
\omega\in\mathcal{S}^{t-1}
independent of
\{\psi_{k}\}
,
\sum_{k}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\frac{1}{n}I_{p})
, therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\circ F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\left\|F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\right\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}^{2},

where the last inequality makes use of (
E.1
).

Lemma 9
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
7
, it satisfies

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathsf{Var}(X_{i}^{0}+X_{i})\lesssim\frac{\log n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}^{2}+\rho_{F}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}).

(239)

The proof of this result is postponed to Section
F.3
.

Putting these pieces together proves the claimed result in inequality (
237a
).

Step 2: sizes control.

To apply Lemma
3
, one needs to check condition (
166
).
Since the zero mean condition holds as in (
236
), it only requires us to provide a high probability control on the size of
H(\Psi,\theta)
for each
\theta\in\Theta.

In the following, we bound the sizes of
X_{i},Y_{i}
and
Z_{i}
respectively.

•

By definition of
X_{i}
in expression (
227b
), we claim that

\displaystyle|X_{i}|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k,i}\Big|\cdot\left|F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\right)\right|_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\cdot\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t},

(240)

with probability at least
1-\delta
, for every
\delta\lesssim\frac{1}{t}
.

In order to see this, first notice that for every
k\geq 1
and
t\leq n
,
v_{k}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\frac{\|\alpha_{k-1}\|_{2}^{2}}{n})
and
\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k,i}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\frac{1}{n})
. Standard concentration inequality ensures that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k,i}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\qquad\text{ and }\|v_{k}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\|\alpha_{k-1}\|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}},

with probability at least
1-\delta
.
In addition, taking the Lipschitz property of
F_{t+1}
together with the above concentration results ensures

\displaystyle\left|F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})-F_{t+1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\right)\right|_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{F}\left|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\right|_{i}

\displaystyle=\rho_{F}\left|v_{t+1}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(v_{k})-\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\right|_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\left[\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}\|\alpha_{k-1}\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{F}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\overline{\alpha}_{t},

(241)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.
Here the last inequality uses the definition (
186a
) and the condition (
191c
),
Putting everything together completes the proof of (
•
‣
E.2
).

•

As for
Y_{i}
, in view of the definition (
227c
), some direct algebra leads to

\displaystyle|Y_{i}|=\left|\frac{1}{n}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right]_{i}\cdot\omega^{\top}\alpha_{t}\right|\leq\frac{1}{n}\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\|_{\infty}\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\rho_{F}}{n}\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}.

•

Regarding
Z_{i}
, it is easily seen that

\displaystyle|Z_{i}|

\displaystyle=\Big|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left[F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right]_{i}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\omega_{k}\alpha_{j}^{k}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\cdot\left\|F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\|\alpha_{j}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\rho_{F}^{2}}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\|\alpha_{j}\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}}{n}\left\|\alpha_{t}\right\|_{2},

where the last inequality follows from the condition
\rho_{F}\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\ll 1.

Combining the pieces above, we are ensured that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|X_{i}|+|Y_{i}|+|Z_{i}|\lesssim\frac{\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{n}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)\geq 1-\delta.

(242)

Step 3: Putting everything together.

Equipped with the above variances control and sizes control, a direct application of Lemma
3
yields that for each
\theta\in\Theta
independent of the problem randomnesses,

\displaystyle\left|H(\Psi;\theta)\right|=\left|\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Y_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Z_{i}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t})

(243)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Step 4: a covering argument.

Thus far, we have established an upper bound for
\left|H(\Psi;\theta)\right|
regarding any fixed
\theta\in\Theta.
In the following, we aim to develop a control of
\left|H(\Psi;\theta)\right|
uniformly over the space
\Theta
via a standard covering argument in addition to a uniform bound.
A direct covering of space
\Theta
requires a covering number of order

O\Big((\frac{1}{\epsilon})^{t^{2}}\Big),

which leads to a squared dependence of
t
in expression (
7
).
Next, we show that it is sufficient to construct a set of cardinality

O\Big((\frac{1}{\epsilon})^{t\log n}\Big),

where function
H(\Psi;\cdot)
lies at most
\epsilon
apart from each other. Implementing this idea leads to the right dependence of
t
in expression (
7
).

Before proceeding, let us denote
\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}
as an
\epsilon
-net for a subset of
\Theta
(referred to as
\Theta_{0}
) where

\displaystyle\Theta_{0}\coloneqq\Bigg\{\Big(\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)~\big|\text{ for every }\Big(\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widehat{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)\in\Theta\Bigg\},

(244)

where for each
k\leq t
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}=\begin{cases}0,&\text{for }k\leq t-O(\log n),\\
\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}&\text{o.w.}\end{cases}

(245)

In words,
\Theta_{0}
stands for the subset of
\Theta
where the corresponding
\widehat{\gamma}_{t}
is restricted to have zero entries except for the last
O(\log n)
coordinates, namely,
\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}=0
for
k\leq t-O(\log n)
.
For every
\widehat{\theta}=\Big(\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widehat{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)\in\Theta
, assumption (
191a
) ensures that
|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\leq\epsilon
for
k\leq t-O(\log n)
.
Therefore, there exists some
\widetilde{\theta}\in\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}
with

\displaystyle\widetilde{\theta}\coloneqq\Big(\widetilde{\omega},\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t},\{\widetilde{\tau}_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big),

such that
\|\omega-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon
, and for every
k
,
\|\alpha_{k}-\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon
,
\|\tau_{k}-\widetilde{\tau}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon
and
|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\leq\epsilon
.
We claim that

\displaystyle|H(\Psi;\widehat{\theta})-H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta})|\lesssim\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}).

(246)

Let us take inequality (
246
) as given for the moment and come back to its proof after establishing our main result.
It turns out that given the structure of
\Theta_{0}
, it is sufficient to consider a subset
\Theta^{\prime}_{0}\subset\Theta_{0}
where the corresponding
\alpha_{k}=0\in\real^{k}\text{ for }k\leq t-O(\log n).

More specifically, define set

\displaystyle\Theta_{0}^{\prime}\coloneqq\Bigg\{\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}=(\widetilde{\omega}^{\prime},\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}^{\prime}\}_{k\leq t},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{\prime},\{\widetilde{\tau}_{k}^{\prime}\}_{k\leq t+1})\in\Theta_{0}~\big|\text{ for every }\widetilde{\theta}=\Big(\widetilde{\omega},\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t},\{\widetilde{\tau}_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)\in\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}\Bigg\},

(247)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\omega}^{\prime}=\widetilde{\omega},~\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{\prime}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{t},~\widetilde{\tau}_{k}^{\prime}=\widetilde{\tau}_{k},~\forall k\leq t+1,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}^{\prime}=0\in\real^{k}\text{ for }k\leq t-O(\log n),\quad\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}^{\prime}=\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\text{ for }t-O(\log n)\leq k\leq t.

Given a
\widetilde{\theta}
and
\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}
pair, since the corresponding
\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{\prime k}=0
, we are guaranteed that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\coloneqq v_{t+1}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(v_{k})=v_{t+1}+\sum_{k=t-O(\log n)}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{\prime k}F_{k}(v_{k})=\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}^{\prime},

(248)

as
v_{k+1}
is determined by
\alpha_{k}\in\real^{k}
. In addition, in view of the definition of
H
function (cf. (
235
)), the corresponding
H
functions have the same value as

\displaystyle H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta})=H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}).

(249)

These observations imply that it is enough to restrict to set
\Theta_{0}^{\prime}
when consider a covering for function
H(\Psi;\theta)
.
By construction, the cardinality of
\Theta_{0}^{\prime}
equals to

\displaystyle|\Theta_{0}^{\prime}|\lesssim\Big(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\Big)^{t\log n},

(250)

which yields a much small size compared to
|\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}|
.

Armed with the properties above, we are ready to control
\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}H(\Psi;\theta)
. Specifically, as a consequence of relation (
246
), we find that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}H(\Psi;\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\widetilde{\theta}\in\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}}H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta})+C\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t})

\displaystyle=\sup_{\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta_{0}^{\prime}}H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime})+C\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}),

for some universal constant
C
.
Here the last equality follows from property (
249
).
Now in order to control quantity
\sup_{\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta_{0}^{\prime}}H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime})
, recall that we have shown that for every fixed
\theta\in\Theta
, (
243
) holds true with probability at least
1-\delta
.
Now setting
\delta=n^{-11}\epsilon^{t\log n}
and taking a uniform bound over
|\Theta_{0}^{\prime}|
ensure

\displaystyle\sup_{\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta_{0}^{\prime}}H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}),

(251)

and hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}H(\Psi;\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\widetilde{\theta}\in\mathcal{M}_{\epsilon}}H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta})+C\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t})\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}),

(252)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11}).

In summary.

Putting together inequality (
226
) with (
233
) and (
252
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}~\Bigg\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}a_{k}\Big[\left\langle\psi_{k},F_{t+1}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\right\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\alpha_{t}^{k}-\sum_{j=k+1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j}^{\prime}\left(v_{j}\right)\right\rangle\alpha_{j-1}^{k}\Big]\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}~\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}^{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Y_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}Z_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}~\sum_{i=1}^{p}X_{i}^{0}+\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}~H(\Psi;\theta)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}),

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11}).

Thus, we complete the proof of the targeted bound (
7
).

E.3
Other auxiliary details for Lemma
7

Proof of inequality (
246
).

Let us begin by considering quantity
\sum_{i=1}^{p}(X_{i}-\widetilde{X}_{i})
with
X_{i}
and
\widetilde{X}_{i}
associated with
\widehat{\theta}
and
\widetilde{\theta}
respectively.
Here
\widehat{\theta}=\Big(\omega,\{\alpha_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widehat{\gamma}_{t},\{\tau_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)
and
\widetilde{\theta}=\Big(\widetilde{\omega},\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\}_{k\leq t},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t},\{\widetilde{\tau}_{k}\}_{k\leq t+1}\Big)
satisfy

\displaystyle\|\omega-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon,

and for every
k
,

\displaystyle\|\alpha_{k}-\widetilde{\alpha}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon,\quad\|\tau_{k}-\widetilde{\tau}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon,\quad|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\leq\epsilon.

We aim to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}(X_{i}-\widetilde{X}_{i})\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}(\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\overline{\gamma}_{t+1})\asymp\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}(\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}).

(253)

As already shown by inequality (
231
), for every

(\omega,\widehat{\gamma}_{t})
and
(\widetilde{\omega},\widetilde{\gamma}_{t})
pairs satisfying
\|\widetilde{\omega}-\omega\|_{2}+\|\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}-\widehat{\gamma}_{t}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon=\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}
,
one has

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\rangle-\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(0)\Big)\Big\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}.

(254)

Similarly, the Lipschitz property of function
F_{t}
ensures that

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)\Big\rangle-\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k},F_{t+1}\Big(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)\Big\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}\Big\|F_{t+1}\Big(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)-F_{t+1}\Big(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)\Big\|_{2}+\Big\|F_{t+1}\Big(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)\Big\|_{2}\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{t}\omega_{k}\psi_{k}-\sum_{k=1}^{t}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\psi_{k}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\mathrm{i})}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{p}{n}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\left(\rho_{F}\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}-\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}+\Big\|F_{t+1}\Big(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\Big)\Big\|_{2}\|\omega-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2}\right)

(255)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}.

(256)

Here
(\mathrm{i})
follows from the event (
224
)
and we leave the proof of (
256
) to the end of this step.
Putting these two relations together yields the claimed bound in (
253
).

Regarding quantity
\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Y_{i}-\widetilde{Y}_{i})
, some direct algebra shows that

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Y_{i}-\widetilde{Y}_{i})\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\cdot\widehat{\omega}^{\top}\widehat{\alpha}_{t}-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\cdot\widetilde{\omega}^{\top}\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\Big|\cdot|\widehat{\omega}^{\top}\widehat{\alpha}_{t}-\widetilde{\omega}^{\top}\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}|+\Big|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\Big|\cdot|\widetilde{\omega}^{\top}\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{F}\cdot(\|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}-\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\|_{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2})+\Big|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\Big|\cdot\|\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\|_{2}.

In order to bound the right hand side above, Lemma
10
provides an upper bound of quantity
\Big|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\rangle\Big|
in expression (
277b
).
Taking this upper bound together with the fact that
\|\widehat{\alpha}_{t}-\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\|_{2},\|\widehat{\omega}-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2}\leq\epsilon
, we obtain

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Y_{i}-\widetilde{Y}_{i})\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{F}\epsilon+\Big(\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}+\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{1,F}\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\Big)\cdot\|\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t},

(257)

where the last step invokes assumption (
191b
) and
\epsilon=1/\mathsf{poly}(n).

It remains to consider quantity
\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Z_{i}-\widetilde{Z}_{i}).

Recalling the definition of
Z_{i}
(cf. (
227d
)), we can write

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Z_{i}-\widetilde{Z}_{i})\Big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\sum_{k=1}^{j}(\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big(\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big(\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big)\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\|\widehat{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}\cdot\Big|\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big|\cdot\Big|\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big|.

(258)

It is then sufficient to bound the two terms above respectively.
First, note that

\displaystyle\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big|

\displaystyle=|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\cdot\Big|\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big|+|\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}|\cdot\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|(\|\widehat{\alpha}_{j}-\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}+\|\widehat{\omega}-\widetilde{\omega}\|_{2})+\|\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}\cdot\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon\cdot(|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|+\|\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}),

where the last step uses the relation between
\widehat{\theta}
and
\widetilde{\theta}.

Therefore, the second term of (
E.3
) satisfies

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}\Big|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widehat{\omega}_{k}\widehat{\alpha}_{j}^{k}-\widetilde{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}\sum_{k=1}^{j}\widetilde{\omega}_{k}\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}^{k}\Big|\cdot\Big|\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\rho_{F}^{2}}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\ \sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|+\|\widetilde{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\rho_{F}}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\overline{\alpha}_{t},

(259)

recognizing the uniform bound
|F^{\prime}_{k}|\leq\rho_{F}.

When it comes to the first term in expression (
E.3
), Lemma
10
controls the difference
|\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\rangle-\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\rangle|
in expression (
277a
);
invoking this bound, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\|\widehat{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}\cdot\Big|\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle F_{t+1}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1})\circ F_{j+1}^{\prime}\left(v_{j+1}\right)\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{j+1}|\|\widehat{\alpha}_{j}\|_{2}\Big(\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{F}\rho_{1,F}\|\alpha_{t}\|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\frac{1}{\mathsf{poly}(n)}\rho_{1,F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\lesssim\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t},

where we plug in the assumptions (
191b
) and (
191c
).
Combining pieces together, we conclude that

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Z_{i}-\widetilde{Z}_{i})\Big|\lesssim\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}.

(260)

To conclude, by combining the three parts above in expressions (
253
), (
E.3
) and (
260
) together, we end up with

\displaystyle|H(\Psi;\widehat{\theta})-H(\Psi;\widetilde{\theta})|\leq\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(X_{i}-\widetilde{X}_{i})\Big|+\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Y_{i}-\widetilde{Y}_{i})\Big|+\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{p}(Z_{i}-\widetilde{Z}_{i})\Big|\lesssim\frac{t\log^{2}n}{n}(\overline{\gamma}_{t+1}+\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}),

thus completing the inequality (
246
).

Proof of inequality (
256
).

First, conditioning on event (
224
), we make note of the following two relations where

\displaystyle\|F_{k}(v_{k})\|_{2}\leq\|F_{k}(0)\|_{2}+\rho_{F}\|v_{k}\|_{2}\leq\overline{\alpha}_{k}+\rho_{F}\frac{p}{n}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\|\alpha_{k-1}\|_{2}\lesssim\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{k},

(261)

and

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}\leq\|v_{t+1}\|_{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}F_{k}(v_{k})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\|\alpha_{t}\|+\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\|F_{k}(v_{k})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{p}{n}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{t\log p}{p}}\Big)\|\alpha_{t}\|+\sum_{k=1}^{t}|\widehat{\gamma}_{t}^{k}|\rho_{F}\overline{\alpha}_{t}\lesssim\overline{\alpha}_{t}.

(262)

Here inequality (
E.3
) holds for
\|\widetilde{\beta}_{t+1}\|_{2}
similarly.
